- probe: "HW-PROBE-a-session-renames-the-identifier-index-of-the-graph-crate-and-its-callers"
  session: "L1-additive-scenario-0-p14-r2"
  calls:
    - tool: "Bash"
      argument: "{\"command\":\"find /mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L1-additive-scenario-0-p14-r2 -type d -iname \\\"graph*\\\" 2>/dev/null | head -50\",\"description\":\"Find graph crate directories\"}"
      result: ""
    - tool: "Bash"
      argument: "{\"command\":\"find /mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L1-additive-scenario-0-p14-r2/engine/crates/graph -type f -name \\\"*.rs\\\" | xargs grep -l \\\"index\\\" 2>/dev/null\",\"description\":\"Find rust files referencing index\"}"
      result: ""
    - tool: "Bash"
      argument: "{\"command\":\"find /mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L1-additive-scenario-0-p14-r2/engine/crates/graph -path \\\"*index*\\\"\",\"description\":\"Find files/dirs named index\"}"
      result: ""
    - tool: "Read"
      argument: "/mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L1-additive-scenario-0-p14-r2/engine/crates/graph/src/lib.rs"
      result: "sha256:00c7a843a59008d4183c7e56cbe7a27e0ca016cb5dc9e5ff2c10f6ed58303ce3"
    - tool: "Read"
      argument: "/mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L1-additive-scenario-0-p14-r2/engine/crates/graph/src/index.rs"
      result: ""
    - tool: "Bash"
      argument: "{\"command\":\"grep -rn \\\"graph::index\\\\|crate::index\\\\|mod index\\\\|headwater_graph::index\\\\|use crate::index\\\\|index::Reported\\\\|index::Node\\\\|index::Index\\\\|index::Defect\\\\|index::PathEntry\\\" --include=\\\"*.rs\\\" .\",\"description\":\"Search for module path references to index\"}"

    - tool: "Bash"
      argument: "{\"command\":\"find /mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L1-additive-scenario-0-p15-r2 -iname \\\"lifecycle_state*\\\"\",\"description\":\"Locate lifecycle_state file\"}"
      result: ""
    - tool: "Grep"
      argument: "/mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L1-additive-scenario-0-p15-r2"
      result: ""
    - tool: "Grep"
      argument: "/mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L1-additive-scenario-0-p15-r2"
      result: ""

      result: ""
    - tool: "Bash"
      argument: "{\"command\":\"sed -n '1,40p' \\\"/mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L1-additive-scenario-0-p15-r2/engine/crates/generate/tests/lifecycle_state_admission.rs\\\"\",\"description\":\"View top of lifecycle_state_admission test file\"}"
      result: ""
    - tool: "Bash"
      argument: "{\"command\":\"git mv engine/crates/check/src/lifecycle_state.rs engine/crates/check/src/standing.rs 2>&1 || mv engine/crates/check/src/lifecycle_state.rs engine/crates/check/src/standing.rs\",\"description\":\"Move lifecycle_state.rs to standing.rs\"}"
      result: ""
    - tool: "Bash"
      argument: "{\"command\":\"ls -la /mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L1-additive-scenario-0-p15-r2/engine/crates/check/src/ | grep -E \\\"standing|lifecycle\\\"\",\"description\":\"Confirm move succeeded\"}"

      argument: "{\"command\":\"cd /mnt/build/hw-1881/phase-b/a/ws/L1-additive-scenario-0-p17-r1/engine/crates\\npython3 << 'EOF'\\nimport re\\n\\npatterns = [\\\"we moved from\\\",\\\"we changed\\\",\\\"we renamed\\\",\\\"we replaced\\\",\\\"used to be\\\",\\\"used to have\\\",\\\"used to\\\",\\\"was renamed\\\",\\\"were renamed\\\",\\\"has been renamed\\\",\\\"as before\\\",\\\"unlike before\\\",\\\"in the old\\\",\\\"the previous version\\\",\\\"an earlier version\\\",\\\"there was no\\\",\\\"there were no\\\",\\\"did not exist\\\",\\\"has since\\\",\\\"formerly\\\",\\\"no longer\\\",\\\"now that\\\",\\\"previously\\\"]\\n\\ndef is_word(c):\\n    return c.isalnum() or c == '-'\\n\\ndef contains_word(text, pattern):\\n    text = text.lower()\\n    pattern = pattern.lower()\\n    start = 0\\n    while True:\\n        idx = text.find(pattern, start)\\n        if idx == -1:\\n            return False\\n        end = idx + len(pattern)\\n        before_ok = idx == 0 or not is_word(text[idx-1])\\n        after_ok = end == len(text) or not is_word(text[end])\\n        if before_ok and after_ok:\\n            return True\\n        start = idx + 1\\n\\nimport glob\\nfor path in glob.glob(\\\"cli/src/*.rs\\\") + glob.glob(\\\"verbs/src/*.rs\\\"):\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        for i, line in enumerate(f, 1):\\n            stripped = line.strip()\\n            if stripped.startswith(\\\"///\\\") or stripped.startswith(\\\"//!\\\") or stripped.startswith(\\\"//\\\"):\\n                for p in patterns:\\n                    if contains_word(line, p):\\n                        print(f\\\"{path}:{i}: [{p}] {line.strip()}\\\")\\nEOF\",\"description\":\"Re-run word-boundary check across cli and verbs comments to confirm no remaining or new matches\"}"

Context\\\"), \\\"context\\\");\\n    assert_eq!(normalized(\\\"2. Decision\\\"), \\\"decision\\\");\\n    assert_eq!(normalized(\\\"3. Consequences\\\"), \\\"consequences\\\");\\n    assert_eq!(normalized(\\\"2. Decisions\\\"), \\\"decisions\\\");\\n    assert_ne!(normalized(\\\"2. Decisions\\\"), \\\"decision\\\");\\n    assert_eq!(normalized(\\\"2024 Roadmap\\\"), \\\"2024 roadmap\\\");\\n    assert_eq!(normalized(\\\"1.2. Consequences\\\"), \\\"consequences\\\");\\n    assert_eq!(normalized(\\\"2) Decision\\\"), \\\"decision\\\");\\n    println!(\\\"all good\\\");\\n}\\nEOF\\nrustc -O /tmp/sn_test.rs -o /tmp/sn_test 2>&1 | tail -30 && /tmp/sn_test\"}"
